# Priority list — missing rules worth fixing first

1. Make “All N tags” open the sibling union: Android opens only the first fandom, omitting works in the other family members (AO3FandomUnionTests).
2. Stop claiming “Last Copy” when no EPUB remains: the unavailable badge ignores the presence of a local file (WorkOriginTests).
3. Prefer fresh remote figures on work detail: positive local words/kudos and local comments/hits currently override newer remote values (BrowseAndWorkDetailRulesTests).
4. Keep the outer TOC title for a shared href: a child replaces its parent, so an Afterword can become a numbered chapter and inflate chapter totals (ReadiumReaderTests).
5. Reject blank/tiny page samples: a complete empty frame or positive but tiny viewport can publish a misleading page count (ReaderPageSampleTests).
6. Show Unknown completion for non-AO3 imports with no declared status: passing the local Boolean directly currently calls them In Progress (WorkStatLabelTests).
7. Supply elapsed/duration estimates to system media controls, including rate-dependent estimates: Android publishes an unknown position and no duration (ReaderTimeEstimateTests).
8. Preserve Comments chapter fallbacks: empty sections and out-of-range chapter positions fall through to work-level comments instead of the tested first/nearest chapter (ReaderSectionTests).
9. Dismiss a removed source-less work’s detail: Android clears local state and remains on a remote-only detail even when there is no remote source (WorkDetailPresentationTests).
10. Count the comment budget in Unicode code points: UTF-16 length overcounts joined/non-BMP emoji and prematurely exhausts the allowance (BrowseAndWorkDetailRulesTests).
11. Preserve semantic note markup: converted note asides lose the tested epub:type="note", although note grouping and prose order are right (AuthorNoteDetectorTests).
12. Provide the separate complete-sentence chunking rules: the default paragraph chunker does not supply the tested sentence-level units or abbreviation protection (TextChunkerTests).
13. Keep the tested status contrast: Teen chips stay yellow in Light and Other categories are gray instead of black (WorkStatLabelTests).
14. Normalize recognized AO3 dates on detail: raw ISO/blurb strings bypass the tested display-date formatter (WorkStatLabelTests).
15. Name queue/collection memberships in their action labels: Android always says Add to Queue/Collection instead of In N Queue(s)/Collection(s) (WorkDetailPresentationTests).
16. Share the Dark background token between reader and app: different reader/subject/Material colors currently diverge (ReaderThemeTests).
17. Give a malformed Chapter section its chapter-1 fallback: Android drops the section label when storyChapterIndex is absent (ReaderTimeEstimateTests).
18. Match creator identities with the tested whitespace normalization and orphan exclusion: direct comparison omits both; the orphan case is a pure-rule gap, not an ordinary sign-in scenario (SeriesCreatorTests).
19. Fill the loading area with bounded prose-shaped skeleton paragraphs: screen-height sizing can overflow a short container, underfill a tall one and shorten interior lines (ReaderPageSkeletonTests).
20. Italicize aliases with real italic forms: Latin and Cyrillic aliases currently use upright Text; CJK/Korean/Thai/mixed-script names correctly stay upright (FandomDisplayNameTests).

# A58 — Reader, Browse and work-detail rules

Read-only static audit. Android: /Users/cidy02/kudos-android-lane at c31f1417feecbc769b98783c38a62b1b98c501b4. iOS: /Users/cidy02/kudos-ios-polish at 76473fc9571798070c0d11ed7cbdc157ead222a9. Paths beginning KudosTests/ or kudos-ao3-reader/ resolve in the iOS worktree; android/ paths resolve in the Android worktree. Quoted spans collapse whitespace and omit full-line // comments; line ranges are inclusive. Kept means source comparison, never an executed test result. No build, network, sign-in or runtime checks. Kotlin tests are cited where located, without claiming they ran. Missing requires divergent code on the Android side; without two-sided evidence the verdict is unsure.


**Coverage: all 463 source tests in the 37 requested files: 451 Swift Testing @Test functions (parameterized tests counted once), plus 12 XCTest methods in TextChunkerTests. Verdicts: 349 kept, 38 missing, 12 not applicable, 64 unsure.** Missing rows share root causes; the priority list groups them into 20 fixes. Shared-helper assertions carry their helper line and, where needed, the call supplying the tested values. CategoryChipTests also contains WarningChipTests, and FandomDisplayNameTests also contains FandomScriptTests; both are included.

Android is right on the checked saved-percentage precedence and unchanged-open/close behavior, current-chapter search grouping, ordinary section classification, rating-ladder/one-shot filters, works-sort URL parameters, required-tag parsing, result counts/ranges, word counting, fandom qualifier parsing/grouping, and session-scoped newest-work caching. **No lost-reading-place or missed in-book-search-result defect is established by this audit.** The unsure rows still matter: notably cross-engine position weighting and typography units, exact layout/insets, legacy bridge sequences, bounded exact-count caching, restore-time indexing, and origin classification.

Representation differences are stated in the rows: native icon families are compared by meaning where possible; inline Kotlin total resolution need not expose Swift’s nullable helper; Readium-owned rendering is not proof of exact CSS/layout behavior. A missing Android test alone is never a missing rule. “No specific matching Android test located” describes this source search; related narrower tests are identified explicitly. No speech-engine or Kokoro implementation audit was performed; only the requested TextChunker and reader timing contracts were compared.

## ReaderPageSampleTests

| Test — reader guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>aBlankPageGivesNoSample</code><br>A blank page gives no sample. | KudosTests/ReaderPageSampleTests.swift:16 <code>#expect(sample == nil)</code> | **missing** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/reader/readium/ReadiumNavigatorHost.kt:244–251 <code>private val SCROLL_EXTENT_SCRIPT = &quot;&quot;&quot; (function() { var e = document.scrollingElement; if (!e &#124;&#124; document.readyState !== &#x27;complete&#x27;) return null; var vertical = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).writingMode.startsWith(&#x27;vertical&#x27;); return vertical ? {offset: Math.abs(e.scrollLeft), extent: e.clientWidth, range: e.scrollWidth} : {offset: e.scrollTop, extent: e.clientHeight, range: e.scrollHeight};</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/reader/ReaderViewport.kt:16–24 <code>fun scrolled(spineIndex: Int, offset: Double, extent: Double, range: Double): ReaderViewport? { if (!offset.isFinite() &#124;&#124; !extent.isFinite() &#124;&#124; !range.isFinite() &#124;&#124; offset &lt; 0 &#124;&#124; extent &lt;= 0 &#124;&#124; range &lt;= 0 ) return null val count = (range / extent).roundToInt().coerceAtLeast(1) val page = ((offset / extent).roundToInt() + 1).coerceIn(1, count) return ReaderViewport(spineIndex, page, count, offset, extent, range)</code> | A complete blank document or positive but tiny viewport passes this sampler; it can display a premature one-page count. There is no body-firstChild or 32px window guard.<br>Android test: No specific matching Android test located. |
| <code>theGuardIsOnTheBodyAndTheWindow</code><br>The guard is on the body and the window. | KudosTests/ReaderPageSampleTests.swift:22 <code>#expect(js.contains(&quot;!document.body.firstChild&quot;))</code><br>KudosTests/ReaderPageSampleTests.swift:23 <code>#expect(js.contains(&quot;window.innerWidth &#124;&#124; 0) &lt; 32&quot;))</code> | **missing** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/reader/readium/ReadiumNavigatorHost.kt:244–251 <code>private val SCROLL_EXTENT_SCRIPT = &quot;&quot;&quot; (function() { var e = document.scrollingElement; if (!e &#124;&#124; document.readyState !== &#x27;complete&#x27;) return null; var vertical = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).writingMode.startsWith(&#x27;vertical&#x27;); return vertical ? {offset: Math.abs(e.scrollLeft), extent: e.clientWidth, range: e.scrollWidth} : {offset: e.scrollTop, extent: e.clientHeight, range: e.scrollHeight};</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/reader/ReaderViewport.kt:16–24 <code>fun scrolled(spineIndex: Int, offset: Double, extent: Double, range: Double): ReaderViewport? { if (!offset.isFinite() &#124;&#124; !extent.isFinite() &#124;&#124; !range.isFinite() &#124;&#124; offset &lt; 0 &#124;&#124; extent &lt;= 0 &#124;&#124; range &lt;= 0 ) return null val count = (range / extent).roundToInt().coerceAtLeast(1) val page = ((offset / extent).roundToInt() + 1).coerceIn(1, count) return ReaderViewport(spineIndex, page, count, offset, extent, range)</code> | A complete blank document or positive but tiny viewport passes this sampler; it can display a premature one-page count. There is no body-firstChild or 32px window guard.<br>Android test: No specific matching Android test located. |

## WorkDetailPresentationTests

| Test — reader guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>readActionNeverSaysDownload</code><br>Read action never says download. | KudosTests/WorkDetailPresentationTests.swift:12 <code>#expect(WorkDetailPresentation.readAction(working: false).title == &quot;Read&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/WorkDetailPresentationTests.swift:13 <code>#expect(WorkDetailPresentation.readAction(working: true).title == &quot;Opening…&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/WorkDetailPresentationTests.swift:14 <code>#expect(WorkDetailPresentation.readAction(working: false, continueReading: true).title == &quot;Continue Reading&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/WorkDetailPresentationTests.swift:16 <code>#expect(WorkDetailPresentation.readAction(working: true, continueReading: true).title == &quot;Opening…&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkDetailScreen.kt:1514–1518 <code>val actionTitle = when { busy -&gt; &quot;Opening…&quot; (state.local?.hasStartedReading == true) &amp;&amp; !(state.local.isFinished) -&gt; &quot;Continue Reading&quot; else -&gt; &quot;Read&quot; }</code> | The visible reading action stays Read / Continue Reading / Opening….<br>Android test: No specific matching Android test located. |

| <code>localWorkMetadataNamesAuthorWordsAndChapters</code><br>Local work metadata names author words and chapters. | KudosTests/WorkStatLabelTests.swift:195 <code>#expect(WorkStat.localWorkMetadata(author: &quot;Rosalita&quot;, wordCount: 1_773, chapters: &quot;3/3&quot;) == [&quot;Rosalita&quot;, &quot;1.77K words&quot;, &quot;3/3&quot;])</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/home/HomeFacts.kt:168–190 <code>fun localWorkMetadata(author: String, wordCount: Int, chapters: String): List&lt;String&gt; { val segments = mutableListOf&lt;String&gt;() val name = author.trim() if (name.isNotEmpty()) segments += name if (wordCount &gt; 0) segments += compactWordCount(wordCount) val chapterRange = chapters.trim() if (chapterRange.isNotEmpty()) segments += chapterRange return segments } fun compactWordCount(count: Int): String = compactFigure(count) + &quot; words&quot; /** 999 → &quot;999&quot;, 1_250 → &quot;1.25K&quot;, 1_200_000 → &quot;1.2M&quot;. */ fun compactFigure(count: Int): String { if (count &lt; 1_000) return count.toString() val (scaled, suffix) = when { count &gt;= 1_000_000_000 -&gt; count / 1_000_000_000.0 to &quot;B&quot; count &gt;= 1_000_000 -&gt; count / 1_000_000.0 to &quot;M&quot; else -&gt; count / 1_000.0 to &quot;K&quot; } val rounded = round(scaled * 100.0) / 100.0 val digits = String.format(Locale.US, &quot;%.2f&quot;, rounded).trimEnd(&#x27;0&#x27;).trimEnd(&#x27;.&#x27;) return digits + suffix</code> | Author/chapters are trimmed, unknown counts omitted, and words compact to up to two fractional digits.<br>Android test: No specific matching Android test located. |

| <code>searchIndexDedupesAcrossCategoriesKeepingHighestCount</code><br>Search index dedupes across categories keeping highest count. | KudosTests/FandomCatalogSearchTests.swift:22 <code>#expect(index.count == 3)</code><br>KudosTests/FandomCatalogSearchTests.swift:24 <code>#expect(marvel?.fandom.workCount == 4_000)</code><br>KudosTests/FandomCatalogSearchTests.swift:26 <code>#expect(index.map(\.normalizedName) == [&quot;marvel&quot;, &quot;doctor who&quot;, &quot;sherlock (tv)&quot;])</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/search/SearchLocalMatches.kt:83–106 <code>if (normalizedQuery.isEmpty() &#124;&#124; limit &lt;= 0) return emptyList() val bestByName = LinkedHashMap&lt;String, AO3Fandom&gt;() for (fandom in fandoms) { val key = WorkSearchIndex.normalize(fandom.name) if (key.isEmpty()) continue val existing = bestByName[key] if (existing != null &amp;&amp; (existing.workCount ?: 0) &gt;= (fandom.workCount ?: 0)) continue bestByName[key] = fandom } val ranked = bestByName.entries.sortedWith( compareByDescending&lt;Map.Entry&lt;String, AO3Fandom&gt;&gt; { it.value.workCount ?: 0 } .thenBy { it.key } ) val prefix = mutableListOf&lt;AO3Fandom&gt;() val contains = mutableListOf&lt;AO3Fandom&gt;() for ((key, fandom) in ranked) { if (key.startsWith(normalizedQuery)) { prefix += fandom if (prefix.size &gt;= limit) break } else if (contains.size &lt; limit &amp;&amp; normalizedQuery in key) { contains += fandom } } return (prefix + contains).take(limit)</code> | The Kotlin search deduplicates normalized names with the largest count, folds diacritics/case, ranks prefixes ahead of substrings and applies the shared limit. | <code>aTrailingParentheticalIsTheQualifier</code><br>A trailing parenthetical is the qualifier. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:14 <code>#expect(split.title == &quot;My Hero Academia&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:15 <code>#expect(split.qualifier == &quot;(Anime &amp; Manga)&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:102–115 <code>for (i in 0 until rules.size) { val before = title for (rule in rules) { if (taken.contains(rule.name)) continue val peel = rule.cut(title) ?: continue if (peel.head.isEmpty()) continue qualifiers.add(0, FandomQualifier(rule.kind, peel.qualifier)) title = peel.head taken.add(rule.name) } if (title == before) break } return FandomName(original = name, title = title, parts = qualifiers)</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:152–162 <code>private fun takeBracket(title: String): Peel? { val last = title.lastOrNull() ?: return null if (last !in closingBrackets) return null val lastAscii = title.lastIndexOf(&#x27;(&#x27;) val lastFull = title.lastIndexOf(&#x27;（&#x27;) val open = maxOf(lastAscii, lastFull) if (open &lt; 0) return null return Peel( tidied(title.substring(0, open)), title.substring(open) )</code> | Only a trailing parenthetical with an opener peels; ASCII/fullwidth delimiters are accepted and preceding title punctuation survives.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:15–17 <code>fun aTrailingParentheticalIsTheQualifier() { val split = FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;My Hero Academia (Anime &amp; Manga)&quot;) assertEquals(&quot;My Hero Academia&quot;, split.title)</code> |

| <code>anAuthorHandleIsDemotedLikeAnyOtherCreator</code><br>An author handle is demoted like any other creator. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:99 <code>#expect(split.title == &quot;Stars of Chaos: Sha Po Lang&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:100 <code>#expect(split.qualifier == &quot;- priest&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:48–49 <code>private val separators = listOf(&quot; - &quot;, &quot; – &quot;, &quot; — &quot;, &quot; ‐ &quot;) private val dashes = setOf(&#x27;-&#x27;, &#x27;–&#x27;, &#x27;—&#x27;, &#x27;‐&#x27;)</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:165–181 <code>private fun takeSeparator(title: String): Peel? { var bestIndex = -1 var bestSep = &quot;&quot; for (sep in separators) { val idx = title.lastIndexOf(sep) if (idx &gt; bestIndex) { bestIndex = idx bestSep = sep } } if (bestIndex &lt; 0) return null val tail = title.substring(bestIndex + bestSep.length).trim() if (tail.isEmpty()) return null return Peel( tidied(title.substring(0, bestIndex)), &quot;- $tail&quot; )</code> | Only the last spaced creator separator peels; internal hyphens and unspaced closing subtitle dashes remain title text.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:94–96 <code>fun anAuthorHandleIsDemotedLikeAnyOtherCreator() { val split = FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;Stars of Chaos: Sha Po Lang - priest&quot;) assertEquals(&quot;Stars of Chaos: Sha Po Lang&quot;, split.title)</code> |

| <code>allMediaTypesIsFoundWithoutADashToo</code><br>All media types is found without adash too. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:190 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;Digimon: All Media Types&quot;).title == &quot;Digimon&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:191 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;Hulk-All Media Types&quot;).title == &quot;Hulk&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:48–49 <code>private val separators = listOf(&quot; - &quot;, &quot; – &quot;, &quot; — &quot;, &quot; ‐ &quot;) private val dashes = setOf(&#x27;-&#x27;, &#x27;–&#x27;, &#x27;—&#x27;, &#x27;‐&#x27;)</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:165–181 <code>private fun takeSeparator(title: String): Peel? { var bestIndex = -1 var bestSep = &quot;&quot; for (sep in separators) { val idx = title.lastIndexOf(sep) if (idx &gt; bestIndex) { bestIndex = idx bestSep = sep } } if (bestIndex &lt; 0) return null val tail = title.substring(bestIndex + bestSep.length).trim() if (tail.isEmpty()) return null return Peel( tidied(title.substring(0, bestIndex)), &quot;- $tail&quot; )</code> | Only the last spaced creator separator peels; internal hyphens and unspaced closing subtitle dashes remain title text.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:177–179 <code>fun allMediaTypesIsFoundWithoutADashToo() { assertEquals(&quot;Digimon&quot;, FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;Digimon: All Media Types&quot;).title) assertEquals(&quot;Hulk&quot;, FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;Hulk-All Media Types&quot;).title)</code> |
| <code>theUnicodeHyphenSeparatesToo</code><br>The Unicode hyphen separates too. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:197 <code>#expect(split.title == &quot;Dreaming of Sunshine&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:198 <code>#expect(split.qualifier == &quot;- Silver Queen&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:48–49 <code>private val separators = listOf(&quot; - &quot;, &quot; – &quot;, &quot; — &quot;, &quot; ‐ &quot;) private val dashes = setOf(&#x27;-&#x27;, &#x27;–&#x27;, &#x27;—&#x27;, &#x27;‐&#x27;)</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:165–181 <code>private fun takeSeparator(title: String): Peel? { var bestIndex = -1 var bestSep = &quot;&quot; for (sep in separators) { val idx = title.lastIndexOf(sep) if (idx &gt; bestIndex) { bestIndex = idx bestSep = sep } } if (bestIndex &lt; 0) return null val tail = title.substring(bestIndex + bestSep.length).trim() if (tail.isEmpty()) return null return Peel( tidied(title.substring(0, bestIndex)), &quot;- $tail&quot; )</code> | Only the last spaced creator separator peels; internal hyphens and unspaced closing subtitle dashes remain title text.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:183–185 <code>fun theUnicodeHyphenSeparatesToo() { val split = FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;Dreaming of Sunshine \u2010 Silver Queen&quot;) assertEquals(&quot;Dreaming of Sunshine&quot;, split.title)</code> |
| <code>aJapaneseSubtitleKeepsItsClosingDash</code><br>A Japanese subtitle keeps its closing dash. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:205 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.split(name).title == name)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:48–49 <code>private val separators = listOf(&quot; - &quot;, &quot; – &quot;, &quot; — &quot;, &quot; ‐ &quot;) private val dashes = setOf(&#x27;-&#x27;, &#x27;–&#x27;, &#x27;—&#x27;, &#x27;‐&#x27;)</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:165–181 <code>private fun takeSeparator(title: String): Peel? { var bestIndex = -1 var bestSep = &quot;&quot; for (sep in separators) { val idx = title.lastIndexOf(sep) if (idx &gt; bestIndex) { bestIndex = idx bestSep = sep } } if (bestIndex &lt; 0) return null val tail = title.substring(bestIndex + bestSep.length).trim() if (tail.isEmpty()) return null return Peel( tidied(title.substring(0, bestIndex)), &quot;- $tail&quot; )</code> | Only the last spaced creator separator peels; internal hyphens and unspaced closing subtitle dashes remain title text.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:190–192 <code>fun aJapaneseSubtitleKeepsItsClosingDash() { for (name in listOf(&quot;Lamento -BEYOND THE VOID-&quot;, &quot;Bad Wife -Mizuho-&quot;, &quot;CAGE-CLOSE-&quot;)) { assertEquals(name, FandomDisplayName.split(name).title)</code> |
| <code>blankInputSurvives</code><br>Blank input survives. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:210 <code>#expect(!FandomDisplayName.split(&quot; &quot;).title.isEmpty)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:80–115 <code>fun split(name: String): FandomName { var title = name.trim() if (title.isEmpty()) { return FandomName(original = name, title = name, parts = emptyList()) } val keepsDashTail = FandomDisplayExceptions.keepWhole.contains(title) val qualifiers = mutableListOf&lt;FandomQualifier&gt;() val taken = mutableSetOf&lt;String&gt;() val rules = mutableListOf( Rule(&quot;relatedFandoms&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.RelatedFandoms, ::takeRelatedFandoms), Rule(&quot;rpf&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.Rpf, ::takeRPF), Rule(&quot;mediaUmbrella&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.AllMediaTypes, ::takeMediaUmbrella), Rule(&quot;bracket&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.Parenthetical, ::takeBracket), Rule(&quot;separator&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.Creator, ::takeSeparator), Rule(&quot;gluedFandom&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.FandomSuffix, ::takeGluedFandom) ) if (keepsDashTail) { rules.removeAll { it.name == &quot;separator&quot; } } for (i in 0 until rules.size) { val before = title for (rule in rules) { if (taken.contains(rule.name)) continue val peel = rule.cut(title) ?: continue if (peel.head.isEmpty()) continue qualifiers.add(0, FandomQualifier(rule.kind, peel.qualifier)) title = peel.head taken.add(rule.name) } if (title == before) break } return FandomName(original = name, title = title, parts = qualifiers)</code> | The port keeps the original name, peels each qualifier rule once without emptying the title, and preserves qualifier order and kinds.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:197–199 <code>fun blankInputSurvives() { assertTrue(FandomDisplayName.split(&quot; &quot;).title.isNotEmpty()) }</code> |
| <code>relatedFandomsIsASuffix</code><br>Related fandoms is asuffix. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:217 <code>#expect(split.title == &quot;Sherlock Holmes&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:218 <code>#expect(split.qualifier == &quot;&amp; Related Fandoms&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:58 <code>private val relatedFandoms = listOf(&quot; &amp; Related Fandoms&quot;, &quot; and Related Fandoms&quot;)</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:118–128 <code>private fun takeRelatedFandoms(title: String): Peel? { for (needle in relatedFandoms) { if (title.endsWith(needle, ignoreCase = true)) { val cutIndex = title.length - needle.length return Peel( tidied(title.substring(0, cutIndex)), title.substring(cutIndex).trim() ) } } return null</code> | Related Fandoms needs the leading conjunction and matches case-insensitively; stacked qualifiers peel in source order.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:202–204 <code>fun relatedFandomsIsASuffix() { val split = FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;Sherlock Holmes &amp; Related Fandoms&quot;) assertEquals(&quot;Sherlock Holmes&quot;, split.title)</code> |

| <code>theMediaUmbrellaIsMatchedInEverySpellingWeHaveSeen</code><br>The media umbrella is matched in every spelling we have seen. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:282 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.split(name).title == title, &quot;\(name)&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:50–56 <code>private val mediaUmbrellas = listOf( &quot;All Media Types&quot;, &quot;所有媒体类型&quot;, &quot;所有媒體類型&quot;, &quot;所有媒體型別&quot;, &quot;Todos os Tipos de Mídia&quot;, &quot;Todos los tipos de medios&quot;</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:139–149 <code>private fun takeMediaUmbrella(title: String): Peel? { for (umbrella in mediaUmbrellas) { if (title.endsWith(umbrella, ignoreCase = true)) { val cutIndex = title.length - umbrella.length return Peel( tidied(title.substring(0, cutIndex)), &quot;- &quot; + title.substring(cutIndex).trim() ) } } return null</code> | All tested English, Chinese, Portuguese and Spanish umbrella suffixes are recognized.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:256–258 <code>fun theMediaUmbrellaIsMatchedInEverySpellingWeHaveSeen() { val cases = listOf( &quot;刺客信条-所有媒体类型&quot; to &quot;刺客信条&quot;,</code><br>android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:265 <code>assertEquals(name, title, FandomDisplayName.split(name).title)</code> |
| <code>aBracketPairMayMixWidths</code><br>A bracket pair may mix widths. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:290 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;BLEACH(Anime&amp;Manga）&quot;).title == &quot;BLEACH&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:291 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;南风知我意(TV）&quot;).title == &quot;南风知我意&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:292 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;第三日（原创作品)&quot;).title == &quot;第三日&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:102–115 <code>for (i in 0 until rules.size) { val before = title for (rule in rules) { if (taken.contains(rule.name)) continue val peel = rule.cut(title) ?: continue if (peel.head.isEmpty()) continue qualifiers.add(0, FandomQualifier(rule.kind, peel.qualifier)) title = peel.head taken.add(rule.name) } if (title == before) break } return FandomName(original = name, title = title, parts = qualifiers)</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:152–162 <code>private fun takeBracket(title: String): Peel? { val last = title.lastOrNull() ?: return null if (last !in closingBrackets) return null val lastAscii = title.lastIndexOf(&#x27;(&#x27;) val lastFull = title.lastIndexOf(&#x27;（&#x27;) val open = maxOf(lastAscii, lastFull) if (open &lt; 0) return null return Peel( tidied(title.substring(0, open)), title.substring(open) )</code> | Only a trailing parenthetical with an opener peels; ASCII/fullwidth delimiters are accepted and preceding title punctuation survives.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:270–272 <code>fun aBracketPairMayMixWidths() { assertEquals(&quot;BLEACH&quot;, FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;BLEACH(Anime&amp;Manga）&quot;).title) assertEquals(&quot;南风知我意&quot;, FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;南风知我意(TV）&quot;).title)</code> |

| <code>fandomGluedByADashIsASuffix</code><br>Fandom glued by adash is asuffix. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:315 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.split(name).title == title, &quot;\(name)&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:48–49 <code>private val separators = listOf(&quot; - &quot;, &quot; – &quot;, &quot; — &quot;, &quot; ‐ &quot;) private val dashes = setOf(&#x27;-&#x27;, &#x27;–&#x27;, &#x27;—&#x27;, &#x27;‐&#x27;)</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:165–181 <code>private fun takeSeparator(title: String): Peel? { var bestIndex = -1 var bestSep = &quot;&quot; for (sep in separators) { val idx = title.lastIndexOf(sep) if (idx &gt; bestIndex) { bestIndex = idx bestSep = sep } } if (bestIndex &lt; 0) return null val tail = title.substring(bestIndex + bestSep.length).trim() if (tail.isEmpty()) return null return Peel( tidied(title.substring(0, bestIndex)), &quot;- $tail&quot; )</code> | Only the last spaced creator separator peels; internal hyphens and unspaced closing subtitle dashes remain title text.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:288–290 <code>fun fandomGluedByADashIsASuffix() { val cases = listOf( &quot;The Expanse-Fandom&quot; to &quot;The Expanse&quot;,</code><br>android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:297 <code>assertEquals(name, title, FandomDisplayName.split(name).title)</code> |
| <code>fandomWithoutADashIsPartOfTheTitle</code><br>Fandom without adash is part of the title. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:322 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.split(name).title == name, &quot;\(name) should stay whole&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:48–49 <code>private val separators = listOf(&quot; - &quot;, &quot; – &quot;, &quot; — &quot;, &quot; ‐ &quot;) private val dashes = setOf(&#x27;-&#x27;, &#x27;–&#x27;, &#x27;—&#x27;, &#x27;‐&#x27;)</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:165–181 <code>private fun takeSeparator(title: String): Peel? { var bestIndex = -1 var bestSep = &quot;&quot; for (sep in separators) { val idx = title.lastIndexOf(sep) if (idx &gt; bestIndex) { bestIndex = idx bestSep = sep } } if (bestIndex &lt; 0) return null val tail = title.substring(bestIndex + bestSep.length).trim() if (tail.isEmpty()) return null return Peel( tidied(title.substring(0, bestIndex)), &quot;- $tail&quot; )</code> | Only the last spaced creator separator peels; internal hyphens and unspaced closing subtitle dashes remain title text.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:302–304 <code>fun fandomWithoutADashIsPartOfTheTitle() { for (name in listOf(&quot;Pizza Fandom&quot;, &quot;Celebrity Fiction&quot;)) { assertEquals(&quot;$name should stay whole&quot;, name, FandomDisplayName.split(name).title)</code> |
| <code>thePrimarySegmentIsTheLastPipePiece</code><br>The primary segment is the last pipe piece. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:332 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.primarySegment(of: name) == &quot;My Hero Academia (Anime &amp; Manga)&quot;)</code><br>KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:333 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.aliasSegments(of: name) == [&quot;僕のヒーローアカデミア&quot;, &quot;Boku no Hero Academia&quot;])</code><br>KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:334 <code>#expect(FandomDisplayName.primarySegment(of: &quot;Marvel&quot;) == &quot;Marvel&quot;)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:30–44 <code>fun segments(name: String): List&lt;String&gt; { val parts = name .split(&#x27;&#124;&#x27;) .map { it.trim() } .filter { it.isNotEmpty() } return parts.ifEmpty { listOf(name) } } fun primarySegment(name: String): String { return segments(name).lastOrNull() ?: name } fun aliasSegments(name: String): List&lt;String&gt; { val all = segments(name) return if (all.size &lt;= 1) emptyList() else all.dropLast(1)</code> | Display uses the final alias segment while retaining preceding aliases.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:309–311 <code>fun thePrimarySegmentIsTheLastPipePiece() { val name = &quot;僕のヒーローアカデミア &#124; Boku no Hero Academia &#124; My Hero Academia (Anime &amp; Manga)&quot; assertEquals(&quot;My Hero Academia (Anime &amp; Manga)&quot;, FandomDisplayName.primarySegment(name))</code> |
| <code>siblingsShareADisplayTitleButNotAnIdentity</code><br>Siblings share adisplay title but not an identity. | KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:343 <code>#expect(older.title == newer.title)</code><br>KudosTests/FandomDisplayNameTests.swift:344 <code>#expect(older.qualifier != newer.qualifier)</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayName.kt:80–115 <code>fun split(name: String): FandomName { var title = name.trim() if (title.isEmpty()) { return FandomName(original = name, title = name, parts = emptyList()) } val keepsDashTail = FandomDisplayExceptions.keepWhole.contains(title) val qualifiers = mutableListOf&lt;FandomQualifier&gt;() val taken = mutableSetOf&lt;String&gt;() val rules = mutableListOf( Rule(&quot;relatedFandoms&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.RelatedFandoms, ::takeRelatedFandoms), Rule(&quot;rpf&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.Rpf, ::takeRPF), Rule(&quot;mediaUmbrella&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.AllMediaTypes, ::takeMediaUmbrella), Rule(&quot;bracket&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.Parenthetical, ::takeBracket), Rule(&quot;separator&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.Creator, ::takeSeparator), Rule(&quot;gluedFandom&quot;, FandomQualifierKind.FandomSuffix, ::takeGluedFandom) ) if (keepsDashTail) { rules.removeAll { it.name == &quot;separator&quot; } } for (i in 0 until rules.size) { val before = title for (rule in rules) { if (taken.contains(rule.name)) continue val peel = rule.cut(title) ?: continue if (peel.head.isEmpty()) continue qualifiers.add(0, FandomQualifier(rule.kind, peel.qualifier)) title = peel.head taken.add(rule.name) } if (title == before) break } return FandomName(original = name, title = title, parts = qualifiers)</code> | The port keeps the original name, peels each qualifier rule once without emptying the title, and preserves qualifier order and kinds.<br>Android test: android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:317–319 <code>fun siblingsShareADisplayTitleButNotAnIdentity() { val older = FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;Doctor Who (1963)&quot;) val newer = FandomDisplayName.split(&quot;Doctor Who (2005)&quot;)</code><br>android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/browse/FandomDisplayNameTest.kt:320 <code>assertEquals(older.title, newer.title)</code> |

## FandomFamilyTests

| Test — reader guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin evidence | Consequence / Android test |

| <code>severalCategoriesFoldToMultiOnACollapsedCard</code><br>Several categories fold to multi on acollapsed card. | KudosTests/CategoryChipTests.swift:27 <code>#expect(chipTexts([&quot;F/F&quot;, &quot;M/M&quot;], expanded: false) == [&quot;Multi&quot;])</code><br>KudosTests/CategoryChipTests.swift:28 <code>#expect(chipTexts([&quot;Gen&quot;, &quot;M/M&quot;], expanded: false) == [&quot;Multi&quot;])</code><br>KudosTests/CategoryChipTests.swift:29 <code>#expect(chipTexts([&quot;F/M&quot;, &quot;Gen&quot;, &quot;M/M&quot;, &quot;Multi&quot;], expanded: false) == [&quot;Multi&quot;])</code> | **kept** | android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/ui/components/WorkStats.kt:387–415 <code>val named = categories.filter { it.isNotBlank() &amp;&amp; AO3StatusTint.category(it) != null } if (named.size &gt; 1 &amp;&amp; !expanded) { chips += WorkStatItem( text = &quot;Multi&quot;, accessibilityLabel = &quot;Categories: ${named.joinToString(&quot;, &quot;)}&quot;, icon = Icons.Outlined.People, tint = AO3StatusTint.category(&quot;Multi&quot;) ) } else if (named.isEmpty()) { chips += WorkStatItem( text = &quot;N/A&quot;, accessibilityLabel = &quot;Category: not categorized&quot;, icon = Icons.Outlined.People, tint = AO3StatusTint.gray ) } else { named.forEach { chips += WorkStatItem( text = it, accessibilityLabel = &quot;Category: $it&quot;, icon = Icons.Outlined.People, tint = AO3StatusTint.category(it) ) }</code><br>android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/ui/components/WorkStats.kt:421–457 <code>val real = warnings.filter { warning -&gt; val text = warning.trim() text.isNotBlank() &amp;&amp; !text.equals(&quot;No Archive Warnings Apply&quot;, ignoreCase = true) &amp;&amp; !text.startsWith(&quot;Creator Chose Not To Use&quot;, ignoreCase = true) } val chooseNotTo = warnings.any { it.trim().startsWith(&quot;Creator Chose Not To Use&quot;, ignoreCase = true) } if (real.isNotEmpty() &amp;&amp; !chooseNotTo &amp;&amp; expanded) { real.forEach { warning -&gt; chips += WorkStatItem( text = warning, accessibilityLabel = &quot;Warning: $warning&quot;, icon = Icons.Outlined.WarningAmber, tint = AO3StatusTint.red ) } } else chips += when { real.isNotEmpty() &amp;&amp; !chooseNotTo -&gt; WorkStatItem( text = if (real.size == 1) &quot;1 Warning&quot; else &quot;${real.size} Warnings&quot;, accessibilityLabel = &quot;Warnings: ${real.joinToString(&quot;, &quot;)}&quot;, icon = Icons.Outlined.WarningAmber, tint = AO3StatusTint.red ) chooseNotTo -&gt; WorkStatItem( text = &quot;Not Disclosed&quot;, accessibilityLabel = &quot;Creator chose not to use archive warnings&quot;, icon = Icons.Outlined.WarningAmber, tint = AO3StatusTint.orange ) else -&gt; WorkStatItem( text = &quot;No Warnings&quot;, accessibilityLabel = &quot;No archive warnings apply&quot;, icon = Icons.Outlined.CheckCircle, tint = AO3StatusTint.gray</code> | Collapsed multiple recognized categories become Multi; expansion names each; no recognized category says N/A. ## AuthorNewestWorkStoreTests

## Triage (Claude, 2026-10-15 21:00)

349 kept, 38 missing (20 fixes), 12 not applicable, 64 unsure; no lost reading place and no
missed in-book search result. Claude confirmed #1 in the code: the family row's "All N tags"
opens only `first.fandom` (`browse/FandomListChrome.kt:275–280`), so works tagged only with
its siblings never appear. Brief **3fl** (Codex) takes #1–#6, #8–#10, #14, #15, #17, #18, each
confirmed first. #7, #11, #12, #13, #16, #19, #20 (media-control estimates, note markup,
speech chunking, colours, skeletons, italics) are left for later.
